Setting: the dissociation replicates on modular multiplication and across depths and normalization variants, and a clamp sweep quantifies the companion's central claim: structure injection flattens the weight-norm delay-law exponent about 17-fold (plain cross-entropy slows $31\times$ per +10 norm units, a lower bound as higher cells are censored, versus $1.22\times$ with the prior). Honest boundary: tasks that generalize before memorizing have no delay to control. Feature-family alignment decides whether a prior permits generalization; invariance content suffices for acceleration without labels; a brief early window captures nearly all of the benefit.

</details>


### [124] [Constraint-Aware Aggregation for Federated Reinforcement Learning in Microgrid Energy Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12763)
*Usman Haider,Karl Mason*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于惩罚的约束感知聚合规则（$w_i \propto R_i - αV_i$），用于联邦强化学习中的分布式能源协调，在不改变本地训练的情况下，相比FedAvg显著减少了违规次数并提高了奖励，在DairyGridEnv及芬兰、德国真实数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准联邦强化学习聚合方法（如FedAvg）未考虑系统级约束，导致全局行为不安全，亟需一种能兼顾奖励与安全性的轻量级聚合策略。

Method: 提出约束感知的聚合规则，将局部性能与估计的约束违规纳入服务器端更新；其中基于惩罚的规则 $w_i \propto R_i - αV_i$ 最为可靠，无需对偶优化或修改本地训练。

Result: 在DairyGridEnv基准环境以及芬兰和德国的真实负载驱动数据上，惩罚聚合相比FedAvg大幅减少违规次数并提升奖励；联合奖励-违规方案可通过λ调节权衡，但稳定性较差。

Conclusion: 轻量级聚合策略能在保持标准通信协议的前提下，显著提升联邦强化学习的经验安全性。

Abstract: Federated Reinforcement Learning (FedRL) enables coordination of distributed energy resources without sharing raw local data, but standard aggregation methods such as FedAvg do not account for system-level constraints, often leading to unsafe global behavior. In this work, we study constraint-aware aggregation for federated reinforcement learning in distributed energy coordination. We propose aggregation rules that incorporate both local performance and estimated constraint violation into the server-side update. Among these, a simple penalty-based rule, $w_i \propto R_i - αV_i$, consistently provides the most reliable trade-off between reward and safety, without requiring dual optimization or modifications to local training. \textcolor{black}{We evaluate our approach on DairyGridEnv, a benchmark modeling multiple farms coordinating battery storage under stochastic demand and a shared grid capacity constraint, and further assess robustness using real load-driven demand profiles from Finland and the German FIELD dataset. Across multiple seeds, penalty-based aggregation substantially reduces violations while improving reward relative to FedAvg in both synthetic and real load-driven settings.} A combined reward-violation scheme exposes a tunable trade-off via $λ$, but is less stable. These results demonstrate that lightweight aggregation strategies can substantially improve empirical safety in federated reinforcement learning while preserving standard communication protocols.

</details>


### [125] [Learning Mechanistic Reasoning for Chemical Reactions with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12771)
*Xingyu Dang,Haocheng Tang,Junmei Wang,Yanjun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了大规模反应机理推理数据集，并基于FukuyamaBench基准测试，通过微调Qwen3-30B-A3B模型，在精确路径匹配上超越专门FlowER模型（8.3% vs 5.1%），证明机理感知训练可显著增强LLM的化学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有化学LLM主要关注粗粒度的命名反应进行产物预测和逆合成分析，易产生物理不一致性和幻觉；而专门用于机理推理的小规模生成模型泛化能力受限。因此需要构建大规模推理数据集并训练模型以增强化学机理推理能力。

Method: 1) 构建大规模反应机理推理数据集；2) 建立FukuyamaBench基准（基于Fukuyama高级有机反应机理教材）；3) 微调Qwen3-30B-A3B模型进行层次化机理推理评估。

Result: 微调的Qwen3-30B-A3B在FukuyamaBench Set A上实现8.3%的精确路径匹配，超越专门FlowER模型的5.1%。

Conclusion: 机理感知训练能显著增强语言模型的化学推理能力，在层次化反应机理推理任务上优于专门的小规模生成模型。

Abstract: Reaction mechanisms consist of the step-by-step sequences of elementary reactions that explain chemical transformations. Learning the mechanism logic is therefore essential for enhancing the fundamental chemical intelligence of large language models (LLMs). The stepwise deduction of reaction mechanism aligns naturally with the reasoning paradigms of reasoning LLMs. However, current chemical LLMs primarily emphasize coarse-grained name reactions for product prediction and retrosynthesis, often leading to physical inconsistencies and hallucinations. In contrast, specialized small-scale generative models for mechanism inference typically suffer from restricted generalization capacity across diverse chemical spaces. To overcome these limitations, we built a novel, large-scale reasoning dataset of reaction mechanisms. Furthermore, we established the FukuyamaBench, a difficult benchmark derived from Fukuyama's Advanced Organic Reaction Mechanism book, to rigorously evaluate model performance on hierarchical mechanism reasoning. Our fine-tuned Qwen3-30B-A3B achieves 8.3% exact pathway match on FukuyamaBench Set~A, surpassing the specialized FlowER model (5.1%), demonstrating that mechanism-aware training substantially enhances chemical reasoning in language models.

</details>


### [126] [AVQ-Attention: Adaptive Vector-Quantized Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12789)
*Winfried van den dool,Patrick Forré,Amir Habibian,Yuki M. Asano,Max Welling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应向量量化（AVQ）注意力机制，通过动态分配码本容量给高注意力区域，在保持O(MN)复杂度下提升精度效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力机制的O(N²)复杂度是计算瓶颈；现有VQ注意力虽降至O(MN)但均匀分配码本容量，导致高注意力区域粗糙量化而低注意力区域浪费容量。

Method: 从少量码字开始，前向传播中识别重要码字并用预学习的子码字细化，对高注意力区域精细量化、低注意力区域粗量化；基于Triton内核定制实现，在Flash Attention的分块计算范式内完成重要性评分、子码字插入和父码字替换等流程。

Result: 保持O(MN)复杂度，相比固定码本VQ注意力实现了更优的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 自适应码本分配有效解决了VQ注意力中容量分配不均的问题，在不增加复杂度前提下提升量化效率。

Abstract: The $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ complexity of attention over $N$ tokens remains a computational bottleneck in transformer models. Vector-Quantized (VQ) attention reduces this to $\mathcal{O}(MN)$ by representing keys with $M$ codewords, but applies uniform codebook capacity regardless of where attention mass concentrates: high-attention regions of key space may be coarsely approximated while low-attention regions waste representational capacity. We propose Adaptive Vector-Quantized (AVQ) Attention, which adaptively allocates codebook capacity based on attention importance. Starting from a small set of codewords, our method identifies the most important codes during the forward pass and refines them with pre-learned child codewords, achieving fine-grained quantization where it matters most while maintaining coarse quantization elsewhere. We develop an implementation using custom Triton kernels that enables the full adaptive refinement process, including importance scoring, child codeword insertion, and parent contribution replacement, to be carried out within the tiled computation paradigm of Flash Attention with minimal overhead. Our approach maintains $\mathcal{O}(MN)$ complexity while achieving improved accuracy-efficiency trade-offs compared to fixed-codebook VQ-attention.

</details>


### [127] [Accelerating Masked Diffusion Large Language Models: A Survey of Efficient Inference Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12829)
*Daehoon Gwak,Minhyung Lee,Junwoo Park,Jaegul Choo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）的推理效率问题，提出了一个统一的延迟分解框架，从算法、架构和系统三个维度系统分类了加速技术，并提供了可复现基准测试的指南和开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型在并行生成方面具有理论优势，但并行生成本身并不保证实际加速。现有基准测试往往混淆了算法、架构和系统层面的因素，导致难以进行严格的效率比较。因此，需要系统性地解耦这些因素以指导高效部署。

Method: 提出一个统一的延迟分解框架，将端到端延迟拆解为算法、架构和系统级因素；基于该框架，从三个维度（算法创新、架构与系统优化、推理时扩展）分类综述现有加速技术。

Result: 构建了dLLMs推理延迟的系统化分解方法，厘清了不同层面加速技术的贡献与权衡关系，并给出了可复现基准测试的规范建议。

Conclusion: 并行生成的实际效率提升需要依赖扩散感知的缓存与复用等专门推理机制；未来需要更统一、可复现的基准来评估dLLMs的延迟优势，同时存在若干开放挑战有待解决。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) offer a theoretical advantage in parallel generation over standard autoregressive models. However, parallel generation alone does not guarantee practical speedups. Realizing this efficiency requires specialized inference mechanisms, such as diffusion-aware caching and reuse. Consequently, as inference efficiency becomes a prerequisite for practical deployment, recent research has actively explored acceleration techniques across algorithms, architectures, and systems. However, rigorous comparisons remain difficult, as end-to-end latency stems from intricate trade-offs between algorithmic, architectural, and system-level factors that are often conflated in existing benchmarks. In this survey, we introduce a unified latency decomposition framework for dLLMs to disentangle these factors and analyze their impact on inference speed in real deployments. Guided by this framework, we categorize acceleration techniques along three axes covering algorithmic innovations, architectural and system optimizations, and inference-time scaling. Finally, we provide guidelines for reproducible benchmarking and highlight open challenges for realizing the full potential of parallel generation.

</details>


### [128] [Verifier-Based Reinforcement Fine-Tuning of Reasoning Models for Thermal Energy Storage Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12856)
*Takumi Shioda,Kohei Terashima,Tatsuo Nagai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）对开源推理模型进行微调，使其能够在建筑热储能调度任务中，仅用30条训练提示就将碳排放从70.5 kg-CO2降至61.2 kg-CO2，接近动态规划最优值60.8 kg-CO2，并且发现推理时计算能力对任务表现至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑需要根据电网条件转移冷却负荷，热储能（TES）可以实现这一转移，但调度需要在存储约束下提前数小时规划。模型预测控制（MPC）和强化学习难以跨建筑规模化部署，因此需要一种可扩展的调度方法。

Method: 将精确离线动态规划（DP）动作值转换为每个候选动作的密集奖励，通过可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）对开源推理模型进行强化微调（RFT），使其作为上层调度器，从文本状态和预测中输出小时级热泵设定点。

Result: RFT将碳排放从70.5降至61.2 kg-CO2，接近DP最优值60.8 kg-CO2。GPT-5无需任务特定训练即可接近DP和MPC表现，而GPT-4o（非推理LLM）产生比无储能基线更高的排放。轨迹分析表明RFT主要稳定了候选比较、前瞻和可行性检查等规划模式。在预测误差和未见过的TES条件下，强化后的规划模式具有鲁棒性，并能迁移到电池任务。

Conclusion: 基于DP的可验证奖励为将开源推理模型适应建筑储能调度提供了一种实用途径，这些结果激励了更高保真度的全楼宇控制测试和城市级能源管理的可扩展验证器开发。

Abstract: Buildings are expected to shift cooling loads in response to grid conditions. Thermal energy storage (TES) enables this shift, but scheduling it well requires planning hours ahead under storage constraints. Model predictive control (MPC) and reinforcement learning are difficult to scale across buildings. This study instead adapts an open-weight reasoning model through reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). We convert exact offline dynamic-programming (DP) action values into dense rewards for every candidate action. Using only 30 training prompts, reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT) trains the model as an upper-level scheduler that outputs hourly heat-pump setpoints from text-based states and forecasts. Evaluation uses a deliberately simple office-building TES benchmark where exact DP is tractable and the optimum is known. RFT reduces the open-weight model's emissions from 70.5 to 61.2 kg-CO2, close to the DP optimum of 60.8 kg-CO2. GPT-5 nearly matches DP and MPC without task-specific training, while GPT-4o, a non-reasoning LLM, produces higher emissions than the no-storage baseline, so inference-time reasoning appears important. Trace analysis shows that RFT mainly stabilizes observable planning patterns (candidate comparison, look-ahead, and feasibility checking) rather than creating a new strategy. Robustness and generalization tests clarify what transfers: the reinforced planning patterns persist under forecast errors and an unseen TES condition and carry over to a battery task, but its different structure limits the gains. DP-based verifiable rewards offer a practical way to adapt open-weight reasoning models to building storage scheduling. These results motivate higher-fidelity tests of whole-building control and scalable verifiers for city-scale energy management.

</details>


### [129] [Contrastive-Collapsed Loss for Flexible and Geometrically Optimal Embeddings and Faster Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12916)
*Blanca Cano-Camarero,Ángela Fernández-Pascual,José R. Dorronsoro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCo损失函数，通过促进类内坍塌和类间对比来学习归一化且结构良好的表征，在OpenML-CC18基准数据集上取得了与SOTA方法（核SVM、随机森林、点回归、交叉熵神经网络）相当的性能，并具有更紧致的类聚类和更快的收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有损失函数（如点回归和交叉熵）在学习归一化且结构良好的表征方面存在不足，如初始化距离最优配置较远、梯度信息不够丰富、类间坍塌的激励不足等。

Method: 提出CoCo损失函数，同时促进类内表征坍塌（intra-class collapse）和类间对比（inter-class contrast），允许神经网络近似几何最优嵌入，实现类间大角度分离。

Result: 在OpenML-CC18基准的多种表格数据集上，CoCo损失在预测性能上与核SVM、随机森林、点回归和交叉熵神经网络等SOTA方法具有竞争力；同时具有更紧致的类聚类和更快的收敛速度。

Conclusion: CoCo损失是一种有效的目标函数，能够在保持竞争力预测性能的同时学习到判别性表征，理论分析和实验均验证了其优势。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce CoCo, a loss function aimed at learning normalized and well-structured representations. The proposed loss encourages intra-class collapse and inter-class contrast while preserving sufficient flexibility for neural networks to approximate geometrically optimal embeddings with large angular separation between classes. We provide a theoretical analysis positioning CoCo with respect to related objectives such as dot regression and cross-entropy, showing that the new proposed loss benefits from closer initialization to the optimal configuration, more informative gradients, and stronger incentives for class-wise representation collapse. Extensive experiments on diverse tabular datasets from the OpenML-CC18 benchmark show that CoCo achieves competitive performance with state-of-the-art methods, including kernel SVM, Random Forest, dot regression, and cross-entropy-based neural networks. In addition, both theoretical arguments and empirical analyses demonstrate that the proposal promotes tighter class clustering and faster convergence. These results highlight CoCo loss as an effective objective for learning discriminative representations while maintaining competitive predictive performance.

</details>


### [130] [Efficient Sequential Calibration with $O(T^{2/3-ε})$ Error Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12928)
*Zihan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效的随机预测器，将SPR-Calibration过程与外部Blackwell式校正层相结合，实现了期望校准误差O(T^{2/3-ε})，突破了经典的T^{2/3}屏障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线二元序列校准问题中，校准误差的T^{2/3}屏障长期以来未被突破，近期Dagan等人首次取得突破，本文在此基础上进一步改进，寻求更优的校准误差界。

Method: 将SPR-Calibration过程与外部Blackwell式校正层结合。SPR-Calibration控制相对于代理条件均值估计序列的校准误差，校正层控制使用代理近似真实结果时的额外误差。通过将总校准误差分解为代理校准误差和残差差异两部分，分别利用SPR-Calibration保证和二次势参数结合SPR-Calibration预测器的稀疏性进行控制。

Result: 实现了期望校准误差O(T^{2/3-ε})，其中ε>0为某个常数，突破了经典的T^{2/3}屏障。

Conclusion: 通过结合SPR-Calibration与Blackwell式校正层，成功实现了超越T^{2/3}屏障的校准误差界，证明了该方法在理论上更优。

Abstract: We study the online binary sequential calibration problem. A recent breakthrough by \citet{dagan2024breaking} overcomes the classical \(T^{2/3}\) barrier for calibration error. Building on this result, we present an efficient randomized forecaster that achieves an expected calibration error \(O(T^{2/3-\varepsilon})\) for some constant \(\varepsilon>0\).
  Our forecaster combines the \textsc{SPR-Calibration} procedure \citep{dagan2024breaking} with an outer Blackwell-style correction layer. The \textsc{SPR-Calibration} procedure controls calibration with respect to a surrogate sequence of conditional-mean estimates, while the correction layer controls the additional error incurred when these surrogates are used to approximate the true outcomes. The analysis decomposes the total calibration error into the surrogate calibration error and the residual discrepancy between the surrogate sequence and the true outcomes. The former is bounded by the \textsc{SPR-Calibration} guarantee in \citet{dagan2024breaking}, and the latter is controlled using a quadratic potential argument together with the sparsity of the \textsc{SPR-Calibration} forecaster.

</details>


### [131] [The Spectrum Is Not Enough: When Context Helps Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13006)
*Mert Onur Cakiroglu,Mehmet Dalkilic,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出，基于功率谱的可预测性指数与"添加上下文是否有助于预测"是两个不同的问题。谱指数对相位随机化不变，而语境信息的价值取决于操作点，且会因相位随机化而崩溃。作者提出覆盖亏缺（coverage deficit）诊断指标，用于区分超越谱结构的信号与二阶线性信息，并在七个基准上验证了该区分方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究广泛使用基于频谱的可预测性指数来回答"添加上下文（如更长回溯、检索插件、预训练模型）是否有帮助"，但这两者本质上是不同的问题。谱指数无法捕捉超越二阶统计量的结构信息，而上下文的价值恰恰依赖于这些结构。作者旨在澄清这一混淆，并提供可操作的诊断工具。

Method: 1) 提出不可行性结果：任何基于功率谱的指数在相位随机化下保持不變，而相位随机化后的序列渐进高斯化，因此谱指数无法衡量超越二阶的上下文价值。2) 构建固定谱和边缘分布的替代对（surrogate pairs）实现隔离控制。3) 提出无标签的配置级诊断指标——覆盖亏缺（coverage deficit），其主项通过模拟预测与线性预测的增益来度量超越谱的结构。4) 在七个基准上验证：窗口键控检索、基础模型、线性窗口等不同方法的语境价值在替代对上的表现。

Result: 1) 窗口键控检索的价值在替代对上从ECL中位数+33%崩溃至-35%（p<10^-40），而所有谱指数保持冻结不变。2) 基础模型的价值分裂为两部分：存活的二阶部分和崩溃的超越线性边际。3) 更长的线性窗口的价值在替代对上幸存。4) 留一数据集实验表明，结构项能预测超越谱价值的符号，而谱指数则预测二阶机制的价值符号。

Conclusion: 核心贡献在于区分了两个不同的问题（谱可预测性与上下文价值），提供了受控比较方法，并给出了一个可供部署决策参考的诊断指标。没有提出新的预测模型，而是提供了方法论上的澄清和实用工具。

Abstract: A growing family of indices scores how predictable a series is from its spectrum. Practitioners increasingly read these scores as answering a different question: whether \emph{adding context}, a longer lookback, a retrieval plug-in, or a pretrained model, will help. These are not the same question. The value of context is a property of the operating point, not of the series. Any index built from the power spectrum is invariant under phase randomization, whereas the beyond-second-order value that retrieval and foundation models supply is not, because a phase-randomized series is asymptotically Gaussian. We state this as an impossibility result and isolate it with surrogate pairs that fix the spectrum and the marginal by construction. We then give a label-free, configuration-level diagnostic, the coverage deficit, whose principal term measures beyond-spectrum structure as the gain of analog over linear prediction. On seven benchmarks the prediction holds: window-keyed retrieval's value collapses across surrogate pairs (ECL median $+33\%\!\to\!-35\%$, $p{<}10^{-40}$) while every spectral index stays frozen; a foundation model's value splits into a surviving second-order part and a small beyond-linear margin that collapses; a longer linear window's value survives. Leave-one-dataset-out, the structure term predicts the sign of beyond-spectrum value where the spectral indices trail it, and the reverse holds for the second-order mechanism. We introduce no new forecaster; the contribution is the distinction, a controlled comparison, and a diagnostic for the deployment decision. Code: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SINE.

</details>


### [132] [TerraZero: Procedural Driving Simulation for Zero-Demonstration Self-Play at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13028)
*Zhouchonghao Wu,Akshay Rangesh,Weixin Li,Wei-Jer Chang,Zachary Lee,Tim Wang,Wei Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TerraZero是一个基于程序化生成的自动驾驶仿真器和自博弈训练框架，实现了极快的仿真速度（单GPU每秒130万步），仅利用真实地图几何数据，通过随机化规则生成无限驾驶场景，训练出的策略在InterPlan长尾基准和Waymo开放仿真代理任务上均达到领先水平，且零样本泛化到未知城市。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动驾驶仿真器在速度、真实性和多样性三者之间难以兼得：回放日志数据缺乏长尾事件，传统仿真器速度不足以支撑大规模强化学习，而轻量级系统又缺少异构代理、多动力学模型和交通规则执行等保真度要求。

Method: 构建了一个可配置的C语言引擎，CPU负责仿真、GPU负责策略推理，通过零拷贝路径实现高速通信；仅从日志数据提取地图几何信息，在地图上随机生成基于规则的道路使用者、信号控制器，并对代理动力学、奖励和尺寸进行逐轮随机化，从而产生无限场景；采用纯强化学习自博弈训练，无人类演示和无推理时的备用规划器。

Result: 在单GPU上达到130万代理步/秒的仿真速度；训练出的策略零样本泛化到不同城市和数据集（包括无监督的左行交通）；在InterPlan长尾基准上首次以纯学习策略登顶；在val14常规驾驶基准中达到最佳碰撞和碰撞时间指标；在Waymo开放仿真代理任务中优于其他无演示方法，与最强的参考锚定自博弈方法竞争力相当。

Conclusion: TerraZero证明了通过高效的程序化仿真和纯强化学习自博弈训练，可以在无需人类演示和备用规划器的情况下，学习到能够零样本泛化且在长尾和常规场景中都表现优异的自动驾驶策略，且同一框架同时支持驾驶策略和仿真代理角色。

Abstract: Training robust autonomous driving agents requires a simulator that is fast enough for reinforcement learning at scale, realistic enough to ground behavior in real-world map structure, and diverse enough to cover the safety-critical long tail that logged data rarely contains. We present TerraZero, a procedural driving simulator and self-play training stack. A configurable C engine runs simulation on the CPU and policy inference on the GPU over a zero-copy path, sustaining 1.3M agent-steps per second on a single server-grade GPU, far faster than existing object-level simulators, while keeping fidelity lighter single-agent systems omit: heterogeneous agents, multiple dynamics models, and full traffic-rule enforcement. TerraZero treats logged data only as a source of real-world map geometry, populating each map with randomized rule-based road users and signal controllers and randomizing agent dynamics, rewards, and sizes per episode, so a map yields an unbounded set of scenarios. Every reported policy trains from scratch by reinforcement learning alone on a compute-efficient self-play recipe across GPUs, with zero human demonstrations and no fallback planner at inference. Policies generalize zero-shot across cities and datasets, including emergent left-hand-traffic driving without explicit supervision. As an ego policy, TerraZero is the first fully learned policy to top the InterPlan long-tail benchmark, ahead of larger learned planners; on routine-driving val14 it ranks among the best approaches and is the safest, posting the best collision and time-to-collision scores. On Waymo Open Sim Agents realism the same recipe outperforms other demonstration-free methods and is competitive with the strongest reference-anchored self-play method. One stack serves both roles: driving policies across dynamics for cars and trucks, and sim agents that jointly control vehicles, pedestrians, and cyclists.

</details>


### [133] [The Seriality Gap in Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13031)
*Jorge Diaz Chao,Konpat Preechakul,Yuxi Liu,Yutong Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 视频扩散模型在多球碰撞等因果链任务中表现随因果链增长而下降，而单球控制实验表明问题源于事件依赖结构而非视频长度。研究发现存在"序列性差距"：视频扩散模型的去噪循环无法提供可扩展的序列计算，而对于确定性视频预测，去噪步数不会增加骨干网络之外的序列计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究标准双向视频扩散模型在处理多物体碰撞等具有因果依赖结构的序列推理任务时性能下降的根本原因。

Method: 设计多球硬球动力学控制实验，与长度匹配的单球控制实验对比；进行干预研究（自回归/分块生成、增加架构深度）；理论证明确定性视频预测中去噪步数的计算局限性。

Result: 多球实验中视频扩散性能随因果链增长而下降，单球对照中退化基本消失；增加有效序列计算（自回归生成、加深架构）可提升性能；数学证明去噪步数不增加骨干网络之外的序列计算。

Conclusion: 视频扩散模型存在"序列性差距"，其去噪循环无法提供可扩展的序列计算，这构成了在序列推理和模拟任务上的结构性障碍。

Abstract: When one ball strikes another, then another, video models should predict the consequences of each bounce. In controlled experiments on multi-ball hard-sphere dynamics, we find that the performance of standard bidirectional video diffusion degrades as the causal chain lengthens, even when provided more denoising steps. In a length-matched single-ball control, where ball-ball interactions are absent, the degradation largely disappears, isolating dependent-event structure rather than video length as the cause. Across intervention studies, methods that increase effective serial computation improve performance disproportionately, including autoregressive/blockwise generation and architectural depth. We identify this pattern as the seriality gap: a mismatch between tasks requiring growing serial computation and video diffusion models whose denoising loop does not provide scalable serial compute. We then prove that, for deterministic video prediction, denoising steps do not add serial computation beyond the backbone, indicating a structural obstacle for video diffusion on serial reasoning and simulation tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [134] [Explaining When PRF Fails: Participatory Auditing for Selective Query Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12098)
*Zeyan Liang,Graham McDonald,Iadh Ounis*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段"审计-自动化"框架，通过用户参与审计揭示伪相关反馈（PRF）对部分查询造成查询漂移（仅20.9%查询受益，25.6%受损），并利用LLM重排序器作为系统偏好预测器自动复制用户标签，将不透明的PRF组件转变为可审计、可解释的检索组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PRF在平均指标上提升检索效果，但隐藏了查询漂移导致的大量查询性能下降问题。现有选择性PRF方法依赖查询性能预测（QPP），而QPP源于相同的排序统计信息，继承而非解决了不透明性。本文认为这是核心的可解释性问题。

Method: 两阶段框架：阶段1进行参与式审计，108名用户对43个TREC DL 2019查询进行标注，识别PRF受益/受损查询；阶段2利用LLM重排序器作为系统偏好预测器，基于可检查的文档证据自动复制用户标签，实现可解释的选择性PRF决策。

Result: 仅20.9%的查询从PRF中受益，25.6%的用户体验受损；避免损害的价值约为利用成功扩展的近两倍。LLM重排序器能够有效自动复制用户标注结果。

Conclusion: 两阶段框架将不透明的PRF组件转变为可审计、用户可验证的组件，不仅解释了哪些查询被PRF损害、为何做出选择性PRF决策，还支持大规模检查这些决策。

Abstract: Pseudo-Relevance Feedback (PRF) improves retrieval effectiveness on average, but harms a substantial fraction of queries through query drift, an asymmetry hidden by aggregate offline metrics. Existing Selective PRF (sPRF) approaches typically rely on Query Performance Prediction (QPP) methods derived from the same ranking statistics, and therefore inherit, rather than resolve, this opacity. We argue that this is a core explainability problem in IR, and propose a two-stage audit-then-automate framework. In Stage 1, a participatory audit with 108 users across 43 TREC Deep Learning 2019 queries shows that only 20.9% of queries benefit from PRF, while 25.6% suffer a degraded user experience, and that avoiding harm is nearly twice as valuable as exploiting successful expansion. In Stage 2, we repurpose LLM-based rerankers as system preference predictors that replicate these user-derived labels automatically, grounded in inspectable document evidence. Together, the two stages explain which queries PRF harms, why an sPRF decision is made, and how the decision can be inspected at scale, turning an opaque retrieval component into an auditable, user-grounded one.

</details>


### [135] [SHEAF: Self-profiled Hardness Estimation from Answer-set Flux for Predicting Query Hardness in Graph-based ANN Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12229)
*Dongfang Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出SHEAF，一种基于查询自身top-k答案集在浅层探测宽度下的变化程度来估计查询难度的新度量，相较于传统的局部内在维度（LID）能更准确地预测每个查询所需的最小波束宽度，在CAGRA和HNSW等ANN索引上比五种基线方法相关性提升最高1.55倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于图的近似最近邻搜索（ANN）使用固定的波束宽度（beam-width）来处理所有查询，但不同查询的难度差异巨大（如SIFT1M数据集上，达到95%召回率所需波束宽度差异超过32倍）。传统用于衡量查询难度的局部内在维度（LID）是静态且几何的，对最小波束的预测能力很弱，因此需要一种更准确的、可部署的每查询波束预测方法。

Method: SHEAF（Self-profiled Hardness Estimation from Answer-set Flux）通过衡量查询在两次浅层探测宽度下，其top-k答案集的变化程度（即"通量"）来估计查询难度。具体包括：(1) 设计一个自画像（self-profiling）估计器，将通量转化为可部署的每查询波束预测器；(2) 开发一个固定探测评估协议，基于所有查询观察到的最小充分波束来评分每个度量。SHEAF仅需两次浅层探测搜索，无需查询时的ground truth。

Result: 在CAGRA和HNSW等流行ANN索引上，覆盖四个不同数据集，SHEAF在GPU和CPU上均优于五种基线度量，在留出数据上的相关性提升高达1.55倍。

Conclusion: SHEAF通过利用查询自身答案集的动态变化来估计查询难度，比静态几何方法（如LID）更有效，为自适应波束宽度分配提供了实用的解决方案，兼顾了效率和准确性。

Abstract: Graph-based approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search is usually governed by a beam-width parameter that trades recall for throughput and is fixed for the whole workload. Yet, queries may not be equally hard: for example, on the widely used data set SIFT1M, the beam that a query needs to reach 95\% recall varies by more than $32\times$. Therefore, serving each query at its own width would help if the system could tell, cheaply and in advance, how hard it is. The prevailing proxy for this difficulty is called local intrinsic dimensionality (LID); however, LID is static and geometric, which makes it only weakly predict the minimum beam.
  This paper presents a new measure, namely Self-profiled Hardness Estimation from Answer-set Flux (SHEAF), which represents a query's hardness as how much its own top-$k$ answer set changes between two shallow probe widths. We design a self-profiling estimator that turns this flux into a deployable per-query beam predictor; furthermore, we develop a fixed-probe evaluation protocol that scores each measure over all queries with an observed minimum sufficient beam. On popular ANN indexes such as CAGRA and HNSW across four diverse data sets, SHEAF predicts the per-query beam better than five baseline measures on both GPU and CPU by up to $1.55\times$ in held-out correlation, using only two shallow probe searches and no query-time ground truth.

</details>


### [136] [Not Only NTP: Extending Training Signal Coverage for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12277)
*Changhao Li,Shuli Wang,Junwei Yin,Senjie Kou,Yinqiu Huang,Chi Wang,Yinhua Zhu,Haitao Wang,Xingxing Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出NONTP框架，通过时间对比学习（TCL）和跨域学习（TDL）两项辅助目标，克服传统Next-Token Prediction在时间局部性和空间局部性上的结构训练信号局限，在工业数据集和公开基准上均取得显著提升，且推理时零开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Next-Token Prediction存在两种结构性的训练信号局限：时间局部性（仅优化单步预测，缺乏对长程行为结构的监督压力）和空间局部性（在多域序列中，目标物品嵌入仅从紧邻的前一隐藏状态接收梯度更新，缺乏来自跨域上下文的显式梯度路径）。

Method: 提出NONTP，通过两个辅助目标扩展NTP的信号覆盖：TCL（时间对比学习）采用BYOL风格的EMA教师网络与InfoNCE损失，将隐藏状态与K步未来轨迹在表征空间中对齐；TDL（跨域学习）对跨域隐藏状态进行均值池化并通过共享预测头进行预测，开辟第二条无需额外参数的梯度路径。推理时两项辅助目标均被丢弃，实现零额外开销。

Result: 在美团四域工业数据集（完整排序任务）上，HR@10较NTP提升+34.3%，较MBGR提升+18.3%；在公开Amazon Movie-Book-CDs基准上，HR@10提升+2.8%，NDCG@10提升+3.7%。在线A/B测试确认CTR提升+1.8%，GMV提升+2.1%（均p<0.01）。消融实验证实各组件独立贡献。

Conclusion: NONTP有效解决了NTP在时间和空间维度上的信号局限，通过无参数的辅助目标显著提升推荐性能，梯度冲突分析被指出为未来研究方向。

Abstract: Next-Token Prediction (NTP) carries two structural training signal limitations. First, NTP optimizes for single-step prediction only, placing no supervised pressure on learning longer-range behavioral structure -- we term this \textbf{temporal locality}. Second, in multi-domain sequences, each target item embedding receives gradient updates exclusively from the immediately preceding hidden state, with no explicit gradient pathway from cross-domain context -- we term this \textbf{spatial locality}.
  We propose \textbf{NONTP}, extending NTP's signal coverage along both dimensions through two auxiliary objectives. \textbf{TCL (Temporal Contrastive Learning)} uses a BYOL-style EMA teacher with InfoNCE to align hidden states against a $K$-step future trajectory in representation space. \textbf{TDL (Trans-Domain Learning)} mean-pools cross-domain hidden states and predicts through the shared prediction head, opening a second gradient pathway with no additional parameters. Both are discarded at inference: zero overhead.
  On a four-domain Meituan industrial dataset (full ranking), NONTP achieves HR@10 +34.3\% over NTP and +18.3\% over MBGR. On the public Amazon Movie-Book-CDs benchmark, HR@10 +2.8\% and NDCG@10 +3.7\%. Online A/B tests confirm CTR +1.8\% and GMV +2.1\% (both $p < 0.01$). Ablation studies confirm each component contributes independently, with gradient conflict analyzed as a direction for future work.

</details>


### [137] [SlimPer: Make Personalization Model Slim and Smart](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12281)
*Siqi Wang,Xianjie Chen,Shaofeng Deng,Albert Chen,Romil Shah,Jiawei Huang,Zhaoqin Wang,Zhang Zhang,Yiqun Liu,Meilei Jiang,Anish Dubey,Moyan Mei,Tongxin Wang,Nathan Berrebbi,Misael Manjarres,Armand Sauzay,Shardul Kothapalli,Aryaman Vinchhi,Kevin Johnstone,Juheon Lee,Gufan Yin,Ziheng Huang,Justin Lin,Mert Terzihan,Yilin Qi,Cynthia Yang,Colin Peppler,Qi Ding,Ruohan Sun,Ge Song,Litao Deng,Parichay Kapoor,Matt Ma,Huihui Cheng,Jiyuan Zhang,Yanli Zhao,Yiping Han,Fangqiu Han,Ning Yao,Arun Singh,Jordan Edwards,Zhengyu Su,Abhishek Kumar,Guangdeng Liao,Ankit Asthana*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SlimPer提出了一种用于推荐系统的紧凑型Transformer架构，通过将个性化排序重构为对统一<用户，物品>知识库的迭代精炼，实现了与用户历史长度解耦的O(N)计算复杂度，并在Instagram Reels和Feed上取得了可衡量的用户参与度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer架构在推荐系统中广泛应用，但其设计前提与推荐任务不匹配：生成式模型依赖逐token的自回归预测，需要维护随序列长度增长的大规模中间张量；而推荐系统仅为每个<用户，物品>对生成单一相关性分数，无需token级监督。

Method: 将个性化排序重构为对紧凑型统一<用户，物品>知识库的迭代精炼。每层选择性查询用户侧原始多模态token、计算显式相关性匹配分数并精炼知识库，每层成本为O(N)且中间表示大小固定。通过仅请求优化（request-only optimization）在所有候选物品间共享用户侧token的单一副本以削减内存。

Result: 在Instagram Reels和Feed部署后，用户参与度获得可衡量的提升，同时简化了整体系统并实现了对10k+细粒度用户历史事件的有效建模。

Conclusion: SlimPer统一了稀疏、密集和序列特征于单一骨干网络，通过注意力机制提供内在可解释性，模型深度与用户历史长度解耦，使得在不线性增加计算或内存的情况下实现更深层的相关性理解。

Abstract: Transformer-style architectures are increasingly adopted for industrial recommendation systems, yet they inherit a design premise misaligned with the task: generative models rely on per-token autoregressive prediction, which justifies maintaining large intermediate tensors that scale with sequence length. In contrast, recommendation systems produce a single set of relevance scores for each <user, item> pair without token-level supervision. Leveraging this observation, we propose SlimPer, which reformulates personalized ranking as iterative refinement of a compact, unified <user, item> knowledge base. At each layer, the model selectively queries raw multi-modal user-side tokens, computes explicit relevance matching scores, and refines the knowledge base, all in O(N) per-layer cost with a fixed-size intermediate representation. As a result, model depth is decoupled from user history length, enabling deeper relevance understanding without proportional growth in compute or memory; request-only optimization further trims memory by sharing a single copy of user-side tokens across all candidate items. SlimPer unifies sparse, dense, and sequence features within a single backbone and provides inherent interpretability through its attention mechanism. Deployed on Instagram Reels and Feed, SlimPer yields measurable improvements in user engagement while streamlining the overall system and enabling effective modeling of 10k+ fine-grained user history events.

</details>


### [138] [MESH: Scaling Up Retrieval with Heterogeneous Content Unification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12392)
*Jiaxing Qu,Yilin Chen,Junpeng Hou,Jinfeng Rao,Olafur Gudmundsson,Sai Xiao,Huizhong Duan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出MESH框架，通过模块化架构和门控偏置校正解决异构检索系统中的缩放偏差问题，在Pinterest十亿级推荐系统上实现新鲜内容14倍幂律缩放指数提升、+5.5%新鲜内容转发率提升及2.87倍系统吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模检索系统为处理新鲜内容和长尾内容，不得不采用碎片化的专用检索模型组合（"动物园"现象），其根本原因是异构检索系统中的缩放偏差——模型容量提升对不同内容层级的收益不均衡，导致运营复杂度高。

Method: 提出MESH统一检索缩放框架，采用模块化架构（将特征空间划分为独立域）结合门控偏置校正，通过结构性归纳偏置减少稀疏项信号与高频交互特征之间的干扰，为稀疏内容提供受保护的梯度路径。

Result: 新鲜内容幂律缩放指数提升14倍；在线A/B测试中新鲜内容转发率+5.5%，漏斗效率提升55%，用户留存率+0.46%；异步服务策略带来2.87倍系统吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: MESH为将碎片化检索基础设施整合为更具可扩展性和生态感知能力的统一骨干网络提供了有前景的范式。

Abstract: Optimizing large-scale retrieval hinges on the ability to efficiently surface candidates across diverse content tiers. However, to capture segments such as fresh and long-tail content, modern systems typically resort to a fragmented "zoo" of specialized retrieval models. This operational complexity is attributed to a fundamental challenge in heterogeneous retrieval systems, the Scaling Bias of Heterogeneity, where model capacity gains do not apply equally across diverse content tiers. To bridge this gap, we propose MESH as a unified retrieval scaling framework that mitigates this bias through a modularized architecture integrated with gated bias correction. By partitioning the feature space into independent domains, MESH enforces a structural inductive bias that reduces interference between sparse-item signals and high-frequency engagement features. This protected gradient path leads to improved scaling behavior for sparse content, empirically validated by a 14 times improvement in the power-law scaling exponent for fresh items. In online evaluations on Pinterest's Related Pins platform, a billion scale item-to-item recommendation system, these improvements translate into a +5.5% lift in fresh-item repins, alongside with 55% improvement in funnel efficiency and +0.46% improvement in user retention. Finally, our asynchronous serving strategy ensures production viability by delivering a 2.87 times improvement in system throughput. Our findings suggest MESH as a promising paradigm for consolidating fragmented retrieval infrastructures into more scalable and ecosystem-aware backbones.

</details>


### [139] [Where Reasoning Matters: Rethinking Latent Reasoning in Semantic ID-based Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12425)
*Shangxin Yang,Min Gao,Zongwei Wang,Junliang Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出信息增益预算分配框架（IBA），通过位置级信息增益分析，将有限的潜在推理步骤动态分配给语义ID序列中信息量更大的位置，从而提升生成式推荐系统的准确性与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义ID生成式推荐中，每个物品由一串语义ID token表示，现有方法对每个token分配相同数量的潜在推理步骤，但不同位置的信息增益不同，早期位置通常提供更高信息增益。因此需要研究如何更有效地跨位置分配推理步骤资源。

Method: 首先通过位置级信息增益（IG）量化每个语义ID位置对目标物品不确定性的降低程度；观察到早期位置IG更高、晚期位置贡献更小；据此提出IBA框架，将潜在推理步骤视为有限计算预算，学习在不同语义ID位置间非均匀分配，对高IG位置分配更多推理步骤，低IG位置分配更少步骤。

Result: 在多个公开数据集上，IBA一致提升了强生成式推荐基线模型的性能，相比固定或匹配不当的步骤分配策略，实现了更优的准确率-计算量权衡。

Conclusion: 基于信息增益的预算分配策略能有效提升生成式推荐的推理效率，未来的方向包括进一步探索动态分配策略与更多推荐场景的结合。

Abstract: Semantic ID-based generative recommendation predicts an item by generating a short sequence of semantic ID tokens, where each token is produced autoregressively. Latent reasoning has recently been introduced to improve this process through additional hidden-state computation before each token decision. This raises a practical question: when one item is represented by a sequence of semantic ID tokens, should each token receive the same fixed number of latent refinement steps, or should these steps be allocated more effectively across positions? We study this question through position-wise information-gain (IG), which measures how much each semantic ID position reduces the uncertainty of the target item. We observe that earlier semantic ID positions usually provide higher information-gain, while later positions contribute less additional information. We further analyze that applying more refinement to high-IG positions tends to bring larger expected benefits. Based on this observation, we propose IBA, an Information-Gain Budget Allocation framework for semantic ID-based generative recommendation. IBA treats latent refinement steps as a limited computational resource and learns how to allocate them across semantic ID positions, assigning more refinement to informative positions and less to positions with smaller contribution. Experiments on multiple public datasets show that IBA consistently improves strong generative recommendation baselines and achieves a better accuracy--computation trade-off than fixed or poorly matched step allocations.

</details>


### [140] [Cheaper is Better: A Discount-Aware Network for Conversion Rate Prediction in E-commerce Recommendation System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12578)
*Ruocong Tang,Yang Huang,Xing Fang,Chenyi Yan,Chuike Sun,Jing Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Discount-Aware Network (DANet)，通过时频变换、分布去偏和监督回归辅助任务来建模商品折扣率与转化率的关系，在阿里巴巴天猫APP上部署后AUC提升1.61%，pCVR提升3.63%，GMV提升2.23%。代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CVR预估方法主要关注数据稀疏性、样本选择偏差和延迟反馈等问题，但忽略了商品折扣率这一影响定价和用户购买行为的关键因素，该因素在推荐系统中受到的关注有限。

Method: DANet包含三个核心模块：1) 时频变换模块，利用傅里叶变换获取频谱以捕获商品长期折扣率趋势；2) 分布去偏模块，缓解由不同购买组合、促销活动以及不同促销周期导致的用户特定折扣率偏差；3) 监督回归辅助任务，建立显式商品折扣标签以提升模型的值准确性。

Result: 离线实验AUC提升1.61%；在线A/B测试中pCVR提升3.63%，GMV提升2.23%。模型已在阿里巴巴天猫APP成功部署。

Conclusion: DANet有效建模了商品折扣率与CVR之间的关系，通过多模块协同提升了转化率预测的准确性，并在实际电商平台中取得了显著的商业收益。

Abstract: Post-click conversion rate (CVR) is a crucial element in online recommendation systems, which addresses significant challenges such as data sparsity (DS), sample selection bias (SSB), and delayed feedback. However, the impact of item discount rate-a key factor influencing both pricing and user purchasing behavior, has received limited attention. In this paper, we introduce the Discount-Aware Network (DANet) to model the relationship between item discount rates and CVR. DANet comprises three main components: 1) a time-frequency transformation module that utilizes Fourier transform to derive the frequency spectrum and capture the long-term discount rate trends of items; 2) a distribution de-bias module designed to mitigate the biases in user-specific discount rates caused by various purchase combinations and promotional activities, as well as periodic deviations linked to different promotion periods on e-commerce platforms; and 3) a supervised regression auxiliary task that establishes the explicit item discount labels to enhance the model's performance in terms of value accuracy, facilitating an effective representation of item discount rates. Experimental results on real datasets demonstrate the superiority of DANet, with offline AUC improving by 1.61%, and online A/B test also shows that DANet achieves impressive gains of 3.63% on pCVR and 2.23% on GMV. DANet has been successfully deployed on Alibaba Tmall APP. The code is available at https://github.com/tangrc/DANet.

</details>


### [141] [Learning to Forget: Satiation-Aware Long-Sequence Transducers for Mitigating Post-Purchase Redundancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12714)
*Yipin Dai,Ruocong Tang,Xing Fang,Yang Huang,Jing Wang,Zhentao Song,He Guo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“饱足感知机制”（SAM）的端到端框架，用于显式建模用户兴趣的生命周期，解决现有序列推荐模型忽视“购买即兴趣退出”所导致的购买后冗余推荐问题。SAM通过双路径交叉注意力、自适应饱足门控单元和自监督下次购买时间预测辅助任务三大组件，在工业数据集和在线A/B测试中将购买后重复率降低超过60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列推荐模型将用户交互（包括购买）均视为正向偏好积累信号，但电商场景中购买行为往往意味着特定兴趣的终止（兴趣退出）而非延续。这一“动作-意图不对称性”导致模型在用户购买后仍持续推荐同类商品，造成严重的购买后冗余。

Method: SAM框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 双路径交叉注意力架构，对已满足意图的历史点击进行回溯抑制，同时从长期购买历史中检索个性化补货节奏；(2) 自适应饱足门控单元，生成时间敏感的软掩码，在购买后立即抑制已满足的兴趣，并在预测的复购周期临近时逐渐“重新激活”；(3) 自监督的“下次购买时间”辅助任务，无需人工标注即可学习潜在的产品生命周期。

Result: 在工业数据集上的大量离线实验和在线A/B测试表明，SAM将购买后重复率（PPRR）显著降低了超过60%。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模用户兴趣的“饱足-消退-再激活”生命周期，SAM有效解决了序列推荐中的动作-意图不对称性问题，大幅减少了购买后的冗余推荐，提升了推荐系统的精准度和用户体验。

Abstract: Sequential recommendation models predominantly interpret user interactions as positive signals for preference accumulation. However, in e-commerce scenarios, a purchase action often signifies the termination of a specific intent ("Interest Exit") rather than its continuation. Existing models overlook this distinction, suffering from Action-Intent Asymmetry, which leads to severe post-purchase redundancy. In this paper, we propose the Satiation-Aware Mechanism (SAM), an end-to-end framework designed to explicitly model the lifecycle of user interests. SAM incorporates three key components: (1) A Dual-path Cross-Attention architecture that retroactively suppresses historical clicks associated with a fulfilled intent while simultaneously retrieving personalized replenishment rhythms from long-term purchase history; (2) An Adaptive Satiation Gating Unit (ASGU) that generates a time-sensitive soft mask to inhibit satisfied interests immediately after purchase and gradually "re-awaken" them as the predicted repurchase cycle approaches; and (3) A self-supervised Time-to-Next-Purchase (TTNP) auxiliary task to learn latent product lifecycles without manual annotation. Extensive offline experiments on industrial datasets and online A/B testing demonstrate that SAM significantly reduces the Post-Purchase Repeat Rate (PPRR) by over 60%.

</details>


### [142] [RecRec: Latent Interests Recursive Reasoning for Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12945)
*Wenhao Deng,Junchen Fu,Hanwen Du,Alexandros Karatzoglou,Ioannis Arapakis,Hangjun Guo,Kaiwen Zheng,Yongxin Ni,Joemon M. Jose*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: RecRec是一个无强化学习的递归推理框架，通过解耦推理与预测、使用多向量潜在兴趣表示，突破了传统d维单状态瓶颈，在四个真实数据集上优于现有推理增强方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列推荐中的潜在推理方法将推理与预测耦合在单个d维状态中，限制了推理深度，且常依赖多阶段流水线和强化学习。如何为序列推荐设计有效的推理结构仍是一个开放问题。

Method: RecRec由Context Compressor和Recursive Reasoner组成，通过两个简单的监督阶段训练。Context Compressor将主干网络隐藏状态压缩为一组潜在兴趣，并使用兴趣多样性正则化器鼓励每个兴趣捕捉用户行为的不同方面；Recursive Reasoner在独立的中间潜在空间中对这些兴趣进行递归精炼，通过深度监督允许推理深度在推理时自由调整。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上，RecRec优于现有最优的推理增强方法。在三个数据集上，其性能提升超越了训练时设定的推理深度。

Conclusion: 解耦的、多向量的推理策略能够将潜在推理从先前的单状态瓶颈中解放出来，推理状态结构应作为序列推荐中值得进一步探索的设计维度。

Abstract: Sequential recommender systems rely on a single forward pass to encode user interaction histories and predict the next item. Increasing inference-time computation through latent reasoning, with the model proceeding step by step before the final prediction, has been recently explored in sequential recommendation with promising results. However, how to structure the reasoning process for sequential recommendation remains an open question. Existing approaches couple reasoning and prediction in a single $d$-dimensional state, limiting reasoning depth and often relying on multi-stage pipelines with reinforcement learning (RL). We propose RecRec (Recursive Reasoning for Recommendation), an RL-free framework that decouples reasoning from prediction, overcoming the fixed $d$-dimensional state bottleneck of prior methods. RecRec consists of a Context Compressor and a Recursive Reasoner, trained in two simple supervised stages. The Context Compressor distills the backbone's hidden states into a small set of latent interests, with an Interest Diversity Regularizer encouraging each interest to capture a distinct aspect of user behavior. The Recursive Reasoner then refines these interests by reasoning in a separate intermediate latent space. Deep supervision lets the reasoning depth be freely adjusted at inference without retraining. On four real-world datasets, RecRec outperforms state-of-the-art reasoning-enhanced methods, and on three of four datasets, gains extend past the training-time depth. Our findings point to a decoupled, multi-vector recipe that unleashes latent reasoning from the single-state bottleneck of prior methods, suggesting reasoning-state structure as a design axis to explore further in sequential recommendation.

</details>


### [143] [ViHoRec: A Quality-Controlled Vietnamese Hotel Recommendation Dataset and Cold-Start Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12946)
*Minh Hoang Nguyen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ViHoRec是一个18,267条交互记录的越南酒店推荐数据集，包含跨平台实体解析、隐私保护和冷启动基准测试，为低资源推荐系统提供了稀疏冷启动测试平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越南推荐系统研究受限于缺乏公开、文档完善的酒店交互资源，且构建此类资源面临跨平台酒店名称对齐、可复现质量审核和隐私保护三大挑战。

Method: 从Booking.com、Traveloka和Ivivu三个平台爬取数据，采用跨平台实体解析进行酒店名称对齐，设计可复现的质量控制流水线，使用HMAC假名保护隐私，并构建基于时间leave-last-one-out划分的冷启动基准测试。

Result: BPR-MF在短历史用户上Recall@10从0.120降至0.065，UserKNN整体表现最强，表明ViHoRec是一个稀疏、冷启动主导的低资源推荐测试平台。

Conclusion: ViHoRec填补了越南推荐系统数据集的空白，通过可复现的构建流水线和冷启动基准测试，为低资源推荐场景提供了有价值的研究资源。

Abstract: Recommender-system research for Vietnamese remains limited by the absence of a public, well-documented hotel interaction resource. Building such a resource is challenging for three reasons: cross-platform hotel names must be reconciled before interactions are comparable; quality must be audited with reproducible metrics rather than ad hoc cleaning; and public release must preserve privacy while remaining benchmarkable under realistic cold-start conditions. We introduce ViHoRec, a quality-controlled Vietnamese hotel recommendation dataset of 18{,}267 interactions between 6{,}832 users and 560 hotels, crawled from Booking.com, Traveloka, and Ivivu. Our contributions are: (i) a reproducible construction pipeline with cross-platform entity resolution and quantitative quality control; (ii) a privacy-preserving release with HMAC pseudonyms; and (iii) a public cold-start benchmark with temporal leave-last-one-out split, data-centric ablations, and dependency-free baselines. On the public split, learned models degrade sharply for users with short histories (BPR-MF Recall@10: 0.065 vs. 0.120), while UserKNN remains strongest overall, establishing ViHoRec as a sparse, cold-start-dominated testbed for low-resource recommendation. All data are publicly available at https://github.com/MinhNguyenDS/ViHoRec.

</details>


### [144] [FAIR GraphRAG: A Retrieval-Augmented Generation Approach for Semantic Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11464)
*Marlena Flüh,Soo-Yon Kim,Carolin Victoria Schneider,Sandra Geisler*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出FAIR GraphRAG框架，将FAIR数字对象(FDOs)作为图检索的基本单元，结合知识图谱与RAG技术，在生物医学数据集上显著提升了问答的准确性、覆盖率和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG方法缺乏对底层知识资源的结构化FAIR化处理，限制了其在医学等复杂领域中实现FAIR信息检索的潜力。

Method: 提出FAIR GraphRAG框架，以FAIR数字对象(FDOs)为图节点（每个节点包含核心数据、元数据、持久标识符和语义链接），利用LLM辅助模式构建和内容/元数据自动提取，由医生和计算机科学家共同设计。

Result: 在胃肠病学RNA测序数据集上验证，FAIR GraphRAG不仅确保了FAIR原则的遵循，还显著提高了问答准确性、覆盖率和可解释性，尤其适用于涉及元数据和本体链接的复杂查询。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了将FAIR数据实践与基于图的检索技术相结合的可行性，并有望推广到教育、商业等其他专业领域。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) addresses the limitations of Large Language Models (LLMs) when providing responses to domain-specific questions. Graph-based RAG approaches, such as GraphRAG, enhance retrieval by capturing semantic relationships within knowledge graphs (KGs). While the FAIR principles (Findability, Accessibility, Interoperability, and Reusability) are becoming prevalent for scientific data management, especially in complex domains such as medicine, existing RAG approaches lack a structured FAIRification of the underlying knowledge resources. This lack limits their potential for FAIR information retrieval in these domains. To address this gap, we introduce FAIR GraphRAG, a novel framework that integrates FAIR Digital Objects (FDOs) as the fundamental units of a graph-based retrieval system. Each graph node represents an FDO that incorporates core data, metadata, persistent identifiers, and semantic links. We leverage LLMs to support schema construction and automated extraction of content and metadata from data sources. The framework was co-designed by physicians and computer scientists to ensure technical and clinical relevance. We apply FAIR GraphRAG to a biomedical dataset in gastroenterology, demonstrating its applicability to RNA-sequencing data. Beyond ensuring adherence to the FAIR principles, FAIR GraphRAG significantly improves question answering accuracy, coverage, and explainability, particularly for complex queries involving metadata and ontology links. This work shows the feasibility of combining FAIR data practices with graph-based retrieval techniques. We see potential for applying our approach to other specialized fields such as education and business.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [Predicting Acceptance and Review Effort in Human and Agent Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12057)
*Kartik Ghanshyambhai Pansuriya,Ehsan Ghorbani,Deepak Singh,Eman Abdullah AlOmar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了在拉取请求（PR）提交时（即评审讨论、CI反馈或合并决策之前），是否能够预测PR的接受概率和评审工作量。基于AIDev数据集，构建了无泄漏的预测流程，使用仅包含提交时信息的特征（文本特征、元数据、仓库上下文、时间信号和轻量级diff统计）。评估了多种经典机器学习模型。结果表明，基于树模型的PR接受预测F1得分超过0.95；但评审工作量预测（评论数量、合并时间）较为困难，表明评审者可用性和团队工作流起主要作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI编码代理开始与人类开发者共同提交代码变更，仓库维护者面临如何判断哪些PR更可能被接受、哪些需要大量评审工作的新挑战。本研究旨在探索在PR提交之初（尚未获得评审讨论、CI反馈或合并决策信息时）是否能够预估这些结果，以支持PR的筛选和评审者优先级排序。

Method: 使用AIDev数据集，构建无泄漏感知的预测流程，特征限定为提交时信息，包括PR文本特征（描述、标题等）、元数据、仓库上下文、时间信号和轻量级diff统计。评估多种经典机器学习模型（逻辑回归、随机森林、梯度提升、极限树、MLP），在混合池、仅人类、仅代理和平衡贡献者视角下进行实验。

Result: 接受预测是可行的：基于树的模型F1得分超过0.95，文本清晰度和元数据是最重要的预测因子。评审工作量预测更困难：评论数量和合并时间仅能被提交时特征适度解释，表明评审者可用性、项目工作流和团队特定评审实践起主要作用。

Conclusion: 早期PR模型可以支持PR筛选和评审优先排序，但应作为辅助工具而非自动决策者使用。

Abstract: Pull requests (PRs) are a central mechanism for reviewing and integrating code changes in modern software repositories. As AI coding agents begin to submit more code changes alongside human developers, maintainers face a new challenge: deciding which PRs are likely to be accepted and which ones may require substantial review effort. This paper studies whether such outcomes can be estimated at the time a PR is opened, before reviewer discussion, CI feedback, or merge decisions are available. Using the AIDev dataset, we construct a leakage-aware prediction pipeline for human- and agent-authored PRs. The feature set is limited to submission-time information, including PR text characteristics, metadata, repository context, temporal signals, and lightweight diff statistics. We evaluate classical machine-learning models, including Logistic Regression, Random Forests, Gradient Boosting, Extra Trees, and MLPs, across pooled, human-only, agent-only, and balanced contributor views. Our results show that acceptance prediction is feasible from early signals: tree-based models achieve F1 scores above 0.95, with textual clarity and metadata among the most influential predictors. Review-effort prediction is more difficult. Comment counts and time-to-merge are only modestly explained by submission-time features, suggesting that reviewer availability, project workflow, and team-specific review practices play a major role. These findings indicate that early PR models can support triage and reviewer prioritization, but should be used as advisory tools rather than automated decision-makers.

</details>


### [146] [AutoTrace: From Patches to Triggers via Agentic Interprocedural Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12058)
*Arastoo Zibaeirad,Marco Vieira,Thomas Zimmermann*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AutoTrace是一种基于智能体流水线的漏洞触发定位方法，通过逐层探索代码属性图并利用LLM智能体决定搜索方向，结合确定性可接受门控确保每个报告的触发器都有显式证据支撑。在InterPVD基准上达到75.0%的VulnHit和80.8%的FuncHit，超越此前最优。同时构建了SinkTrace-Bench数据集，包含1,542个经验证器确认的完美平衡的漏洞/安全样本对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漏洞触发定位比二分类漏洞检测更难，因为需要跨过程、因果推理，而大量真实CVE的触发语句位于补丁函数之外多层调用处，超出了静态规则集和模式匹配语言模型的能力范围。

Method: 提出AutoTrace智能体流水线：1) 逐层探索代码属性图；2) LLM智能体决定下一步搜索方向；3) 确定性可接受门控（admissibility gates）决定报告触发器所需的证据要求；4) 智能体不能仅凭自身权威报告触发器，每个报告都必须有来自属性图的显式证据支持。

Result: 在完整InterPVD基准上，AutoTrace达到75.0%的VulnHit和80.8%的FuncHit，超越此前最优方法。基于相同机制构建的SinkTrace-Bench数据集包含1,542个经验证器确认的平衡样本。

Conclusion: 前沿LLM在SinkTrace-Bench上难以区分匹配的漏洞/安全样本对，暴露了因果推理能力的差距，这正是触发定位所要解决的核心问题。

Abstract: Given a vulnerability-fixing commit, trigger localization asks which specific statement turns the vulnerable program state into a concrete unsafe operation. This question is harder than binary vulnerability detection because the answer demands interprocedural, causal reasoning: in a substantial fraction of real-world CVEs the triggering statement lies several call layers outside the patched function, beyond the reach of static rule sets and pattern-matching language models alike. We present AutoTrace, an agentic pipeline that localizes vulnerability triggers by exploring a code property graph layer by layer, with LLM agents deciding where to look next and deterministic admissibility gates deciding what evidence is required before a trigger can be reported. Agents never accept a trigger on their own authority; every reported trigger is backed by explicit evidence drawn from the graph, so the pipeline covers both intra- and interprocedural vulnerabilities without relying on ungrounded model judgment. On the full InterPVD benchmark, AutoTrace reaches 75.0% VulnHit and 80.8% FuncHit, surpassing the prior state of the art on the same corpus. Building on the same machinery, we construct SinkTrace-Bench, a dataset that exposes each vulnerability as a source-to-sink (S2S) causal chain from attacker-controlled input through propagation to the dangerous operation, drawn from matched vulnerable and patched program states. It comprises 1,542 verifier-confirmed, perfectly balanced vulnerable/safe samples whose label fidelity we audit against expert annotations. Benchmarking frontier LLMs on it, we find that even the strongest struggle to separate the matched pairs, exposing the causal-reasoning gap that trigger localization targets. Artifact available at https://github.com/Erroristotle/AutoTrace.

</details>


### [147] [Beyond Test Presence: Assessing the Quality and Robustness of Agent-Generated Tests in Open-Source Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12068)
*Preet Jhanglani,Zeel Kaushal Desai,Vidhi Kansara,Eman Abdullah AlOmar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实证比较（204,673个测试工件），发现AI编码代理在边缘用例覆盖方面优于人类（多样性得分0.62 vs 0.32，空安全测试13.40% vs 8.3%），但人类在断言强度上略胜一筹（88.1% vs 85.37%）。然而，AI生成的测试由于依赖文件I/O和非确定性逻辑，具有更高的脆弱性风险（候选率0.41 vs 0.30），表明AI代理缺乏编写稳定、封闭测试的"环境感知能力"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估基准（如SWE-bench）主要关注测试通过率而非测试内在质量，忽视了测试套件可能存在的"隐性技术债务"——即测试通过但缺乏全面覆盖或语义价值。

Method: 基于AIDev数据集，对204,673个测试工件（24,941个人类编写文件与179,732个代理生成文件）进行大规模实证比较；使用Python ast模块进行抽象语法树（AST）解析，实现白盒静态分析框架，评估断言强度（RQ1）、边缘用例覆盖（RQ2）和脆弱性潜力（RQ3）三个质量维度。

Result: AI代理在边缘用例覆盖方面优于人类（多样性得分0.62 vs 0.32，空安全测试频率13.40% vs 8.3%）；人类在断言强度上略占优势（88.1%强断言 vs 85.37%）；AI生成测试因依赖文件I/O和非确定性逻辑，脆弱性风险更高（候选率0.41 vs 0.30）。

Conclusion: AI代理擅长严格的边界测试，但缺乏编写稳定、封闭测试所需的"环境感知能力"，导致测试脆弱性风险显著增加。

Abstract: The integration of AI-powered coding agents into Continuous Integration/Continuous Delivery (CI/CD) pipelines has fundamentally altered how software verification is conducted. While these agents successfully automate the test generation, current evaluation benchmarks (e.g., SWE-bench) largely focus on pass-rates rather than the intrinsic quality of the generated tests. This raises the possibility of "stealth technical debt", in which test suites pass execution but do not offer comprehensive coverage or semantic value. We address this methodological gap through a large-scale, empirical comparison of 204,673 test artifacts which comprises of 24,941 human-authored files and 179,732 agent-generated files; sourced from the AIDev dataset. Using the Abstract Syntax Tree (AST) parsing with Python's naive ast module, we implemented a "white-box" static analysis framework to evaluate three quality dimensions: Assertion Strength (RQ1), Edge-Case Coverage (RQ2), and Flakiness Potential (RQ3). Our results present a nuanced inversion of traditional assumptions. AI agents performed better than humans in Edge-Case Coverage, with almost twice the variety of boundary checks (Variety Score: 0.62 vs 0.32) and a higher frequency of null-safety testing (13.40% vs. 8.3%), even though human developers had a slight advantage in Assertion Strength (88.1% strong assertions vs. 85.37% for agents). But this thoroughness comes at a price: due mostly to their reliance on file I/O and non-deterministic logic, agent-generated tests exhibited a higher risk of flakiness (Candidate Rate: 0.41 vs. 0.30). These findings suggest that while AI agents excel at rigorous boundary testing, they lack the "environmental awareness" needed to write stable, hermetic tests.

</details>


### [148] [Fault Injection in OpenAPI Specifications for Evaluating Black-Box Testing Effectiveness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12101)
*Hamza Bin Mazhar,Yuqing Wang,Mika V. Mäntylä*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了OpenAPI规范故障对微服务黑盒测试的影响。通过建立六类规范故障的分类体系，在五个严重级别注入故障，使用三个测试工具对两个微服务基准系统进行实验。结果表明规范故障会导致测试工具性能出现强烈且异质的退化模式：方法语义故障影响全面，响应码修改影响微弱，而模式约束松弛则造成隐蔽退化（不影响代码和规范覆盖率，但显著降低请求/响应质量）。研究建议评估黑盒测试效果时需补充请求/响应质量和行为多样性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OpenAPI规范常存在不完整、不一致或错误，但多数研究假设规范正确并以此评估工具性能，忽视了规范故障对测试效果的潜在影响。

Method: 建立六类规范故障分类体系，在五个严重级别注入故障，利用EvoMaster、RESTler、Schemathesis三个测试工具，在TrainTicket和SocialNetwork两个微服务基准系统上评估。通过代码覆盖率、规范覆盖率、请求/响应质量、行为多样性四个指标度量影响。

Result: 规范故障导致测试工具出现强烈且异质的退化模式。方法语义故障全面影响所有指标，响应码修改影响微弱，模式约束松弛对代码和规范覆盖率无影响但显著降低请求/响应质量。

Conclusion: 规范质量直接影响黑盒API测试效果；仅用代码和规范覆盖率评估会低估规范故障的影响，应补充请求/响应质量和行为多样性指标。

Abstract: OpenAPI specifications are the primary input for black-box testing tools in microservice systems (MSS), yet prior work shows these specifications are often incomplete, inconsistent, or incorrect. Despite this, most studies on OpenAPI-based black-box testing assume correct specifications and evaluate tool performance. We address this gap by introducing a literature-grounded taxonomy of six OpenAPI specification fault classes. We inject faults at five severity levels, and evaluate the resulting mutated specifications on two microservice benchmarks, TrainTicket and SocialNetwork, using three testing tools: EvoMaster, RESTler, and Schemathesis. We measure the impact of these faults using code coverage, specification coverage, request/response quality, and behavioral diversity. Our results show that specification faults cause strong and heterogeneous degradation patterns across testing tools and systems. Faults in method semantics cause broad degradation across all metrics, while others, such as modifications to response codes, remain weak. Relaxations of schema constraints cause hidden degradation, with no impact on code and specification coverage but a large impact on request/response quality. These findings demonstrate that specification quality directly shapes black-box API testing effectiveness. Also, code and specification coverage-only evaluations can understate the impact of specification faults on black-box testing in MSS and should be complemented by request/response quality and behavioral diversity.

</details>


### [149] [TraceSynth: Generating Production-Quality Kernel Traces with Constraint-Guided Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12104)
*Yuvraj Sehgal,Sneh Patel,Mahsa Panahandeh,Naser Ezzati-Jivan,Francois Tetreault*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TraceSynth是一个基于扩散模型的框架，用于生成合成内核执行轨迹，通过约束引导修复和Transformer去噪扩散过程，在计算密集型负载下合成数据增强达到87.2% F1-Macro，仅比真实数据低2.6个百分点，上下文长度是主要质量因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业系统中采集生产环境内核执行轨迹的成本高昂，包括运行时开销、存储需求和隐私约束，导致可用于下游ML任务的真实数据有限，需要合成数据生成方法来增强训练数据。

Method: 将内核执行轨迹建模为多通道序列（事件类型、时间戳、CPU亲和性、线程标识符、进程元数据），采用基于Transformer的去噪扩散过程，并引入约束引导修复机制以强制系统不变性。

Result: 在六个基准测试中，scimark2负载下合成数据增强达到87.2% F1-Macro（上下文长度L=4096），仅比纯真实数据低2.6个百分点；L=4096相比L=256相对提升104%；约束引导修复提升合成数据质量最高4.3%；轻量级2通道模型保留全6通道模型97-99%性能，计算成本减半。

Conclusion: TraceSynth能够有效生成合成内核轨迹用于生产可观测性管线的数据增强，并能识别合成数据何时可替代有限的真实轨迹，上下文长度是主导质量因素。

Abstract: Machine learning models for system diagnostics rely on kernel execution traces to capture fine-grained system behavior, but collecting production traces in industrial systems is costly due to runtime overhead, storage demands, and privacy constraints. We present TraceSynth, a diffusion-based framework for generating synthetic kernel traces that augment limited real data for downstream ML tasks. TraceSynth models traces as multi-channel sequences (event types, timestamps, CPU affinity, thread identifiers, and process metadata) using a Transformer-based denoising diffusion process with constraint-guided repair to enforce system invariants. Across six benchmarks, results show strong workload dependence. For deterministic, compute-heavy workloads (scimark2), synthetic augmentation achieves 87.2% F1-Macro at context length L=4096, only 2.6 percentage points below real-only baselines. Context length is the dominant quality factor, with L=4096 yielding a +104% relative improvement over L=256, while constraint-guided repair improves synthetic data quality by up to 4.3%. Ablation studies show that lightweight 2-channel models retain 97-99% of the performance of full 6-channel models at roughly half the computational cost. TraceSynth supports cost-effective augmentation of kernel execution traces in production observability pipelines and helps identify when synthetic data can substitute for limited real traces.

</details>


### [150] [TestMiner: Software Testing Analysis for GitHub Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12223)
*Andre Hora,José Miguel Rojas,Romain Robbes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了TestMiner，一个用于探索GitHub仓库中软件测试实践的工具，能够提供测试统计、测试位置、跨版本测试指标以及测试相关依赖等信息。该工具在本科软件测试课程中由50名学生使用，获得了积极反馈，帮助学生探索了测试组织、测试演化、测试夹具、模拟和边界测试等多种实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同软件项目的测试特点各异（如单元测试与端到端测试的侧重不同），且不同生态系统（Python/Java与Go/Rust）的测试组织方式存在差异（专用文件夹 vs. 与源码共存），这使得理解项目如何组织测试变得困难。

Method: 开发了TestMiner工具，能够分析GitHub仓库中的测试实践，提供测试统计、测试位置分布、跨版本测试指标变化以及测试相关依赖信息的概览。

Result: 在本科软件测试课程中，50名学生使用TestMiner探索了真实GitHub仓库的测试实践。学生反馈积极，能够批判性地探索包括测试组织、测试演化、测试夹具、模拟和边界测试在内的多种测试实践。

Conclusion: TestMiner能够有效帮助学生理解和探索真实项目中的软件测试实践，为软件测试教育与研究提供了有价值的工具支持。

Abstract: Software systems have unique testing characteristics. Some projects can emphasize unit tests, while others may focus on end-to-end testing. Test organization may vary across ecosystems: in languages like Python and Java, tests are typically placed in dedicated folders, whereas Go and Rust projects commonly co-locate tests with source code. These distinctions make it harder to understand how a project approaches testing. In this paper, we present TestMiner, a tool for exploring software testing in GitHub repositories. TestMiner provides an overview of a project's testing practices, including test statistics, test location, test metrics across releases, and dependencies related to testing. We used TestMiner in an undergraduate Software Testing course, where 50 students explored the testing practices of real-world GitHub repositories. Overall, students expressed positive feedback regarding TestMiner. They were able to critically explore a variety of testing practices, including test organization, test evolution, test fixtures, mocking, and edge-case testing. TestMiner is available at: https://andrehora.github.io/testminer. Screencast: https://youtu.be/w1sBgLTq-7Y.

</details>


### [151] [WIP: Software Engineering Competencies in the Age of AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12259)
*Lynn Vonderhaar,Massood Towhidnejad,Omar Ochoa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文回顾了行业对AI需求的文献，提议在软件工程能力模型中新增"AI素养"和"AI开发"两个 competencies，并分析了所需的技能领域，以解决大学课程与快速发展的行业AI需求之间的脱节问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行业AI需求快速演进，而大学课程指南和能力模型更新滞后，未能及时反映行业实际需求，导致毕业生技能与行业要求不匹配。

Method: 通过文献综述分析行业对AI的需求，基于此在SWECOM模型中提出两个新能力领域（AI素养和AI开发），并推导出相应的必备技能领域。

Result: 提出了两个新AI相关能力（AI素养和AI开发）及其所需的技能领域，作为对SWECOM模型的扩展。

Conclusion: 需要将AI素养和AI开发能力纳入软件工程能力模型，以缩小大学教育与行业快速变化的AI需求之间的差距。

Abstract: A university education is meant to fully prepare graduates to enter and succeed in the workforce in their field. Accreditation bodies and professional organizations help to achieve this by publishing curriculum content, skills and competency models to outline the basics that universities must teach within a degree program. Although these curriculum guidelines and competency models are periodically updated to align university curricula with current industry needs, the needs of industry evolve rapidly and are often not properly reflected within the model, a situation which is occurring now with the rise of Artificial Intelligence (AI) use in industry. This paper reviews literature describing the AI needs within industry to motivate two new AI competencies, AI Literacy and AI development, within the Software Engineering Competency Model (SWECOM). This work then analyzes the industry needs described in the literature to propose the necessary skill areas for these new competencies.

</details>


### [152] [Code-MUE: Measuring Code LLMs' Uncertainty through Execution-based Semantic Interaction Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12273)
*Xiaoning Ren,Yinxing Xue,Lei Ma,Yuheng Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出Code-MUE框架，通过执行代码构建语义交互图，计算冯·诺依曼熵来量化代码LLM输出的全局语义多样性，实现纯黑盒的不确定性估计，在8个模型上达到-0.98的斯皮尔曼相关系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性估计方法存在关键空白：白盒/灰盒方法不适用于闭源模型，而标准黑盒文本度量无法捕捉代码特有的脆弱性——语法变化不一定意味着语义差异，即语法-语义鸿沟。

Method: 提出Code-MUE纯黑盒框架，通过执行代码构建基于语义的交互图(Semantic Interaction Graphs)，计算解空间的冯·诺依曼熵来量化全局语义多样性，从而将不确定性锚定在可观察的运行时行为上。

Result: 在8个最先进的LLM上进行大规模实证研究，Code-MUE与功能正确性之间达到强负相关（斯皮尔曼相关系数高达-0.98），显著优于基于词汇和嵌入的基线方法。

Conclusion: Code-MUE能够实现稳健的风险检测和选择性预测，有效应用于实际工作流程中，弥补了代码LLM不确定性估计的语法-语义鸿沟。

Abstract: As Code Large Language Models (LLMs) become central to modern software engineering, their inherent stochasticity poses significant real-world risks, where even minor errors can lead to severe functional, security, or safety consequences. Reliable automation, therefore, demands the ability to distinguish between confident, well-supported predictions and stochastic guessing. However, existing uncertainty estimation methods face a critical gap: white and grey-box techniques are often inapplicable to closed-source models, while standard "black-box" text metrics fail to capture the unique fragility of code, where syntactic variation does not always imply semantic divergence. To bridge this syntax-semantics gap, we introduce Code-MUE, a purely black-box framework that measures uncertainty through execution-based Semantic Interaction Graphs. Unlike prior approaches that rely on superficial textual similarity, Code-MUE grounds uncertainty in observable runtime behavior, calculating the Von Neumann entropy of the solution space to quantify global semantic diversity. A large-scale empirical study across eight state-of-the-art LLMs demonstrates that Code-MUE achieves a strong negative correlation with functional correctness (Spearman's correlation up to -0.98), significantly outperforming lexical and embedding-based baselines while enabling robust risk detection and selective prediction in practical workflows.

</details>


### [153] [XScientist: A Git-Like Research Protocol for Long-Running Autonomous Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12301)
*Jixiang Luo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: XScientist是一个类git的研究协议和操作系统，将自主科研从一次性论文生成转变为可持续观察、可审计的流水线，通过Agent-Native Research Artifact (ARA)协议记录完整的探索DAG、代码、输出、声明-证据锚点及重执行钩子，使每篇生成论文成为可检查的科学探索树。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主研究系统通常被当作一次性论文生成器来评估，隐藏了实际科研中长时间运行、分支探索、频繁失败以及需要代理与人类之间可审计交接的操作问题，导致这类系统难以被信任。

Method: 提出XScientist——一个类git的研究协议和操作系统，核心设计是Agent-Native Research Artifact (ARA)协议，记录探索DAG、节点级代码与输出、声明-证据锚点、内容哈希、溯源信息及重执行钩子。包含确定性完整性取证、样本门控、真相合约、审稿人导向的修复循环及长期守护进程控制。

Result: 实现了将每次运行作为便携式研究工件而非仅作为PDF导出的能力；每个生成论文成为可检查的科学探索树，包含失败分支、修复实验、消融分析和稿件声明，均与产生它们的节点相连。

Conclusion: XScientist提供了将自主科学从单次演示转向可复现、可审查、可复刻的研究基础设施所需的实际保障机制和架构设计。

Abstract: Autonomous research systems are often evaluated as one-shot paper generators: given a topic, they produce a manuscript and a small set of experiment logs. This framing hides the operational problem that makes such systems difficult to trust: research is long-running, branching, failure-prone, and dependent on auditable handoffs between agents and humans. XScientist is a git-like research protocol and operating system for this setting. It orchestrates idea generation, experiment execution, manuscript drafting, self-review, repair, quality gating, daemon scheduling, and reproducibility artifacts as one continuously observable pipeline. The central design choice is to treat each run as a portable research artifact rather than only as a PDF. XScientist exports an Agent-Native Research Artifact (ARA), a protocol that records an exploration DAG, per-node code and outputs, claim-to-evidence anchors, content hashes, provenance, and re-execution hooks. This makes each generated paper inspectable as a science exploration tree: failed branches, repaired experiments, ablations, and manuscript claims remain connected to the nodes that produced them. The system also includes deterministic integrity forensics, sample gates, truth contracts, reviewer-oriented repair loops, and long-running daemon controls. This paper describes the current XScientist architecture, the ARA protocol surface, and the practical safeguards needed to move autonomous science from single-run demos toward reproducible, reviewable, and forkable research infrastructure. The implementation and manuscript source are maintained in the public GitHub repository at https://github.com/smileformylove/XScientist.

</details>


### [154] [How Many Interviews Are Enough in a Software Engineering Study? Preliminary Findings on Sample Size and Saturation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12326)
*Ronnie de Souza Santos,Italo Santos,Mauricio Rodrigues,Cleyton Magalhaes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文分析了2016-2025年间软件工程领域基于访谈的实证研究中样本充分性和饱和度的操作方法，发现样本量从少到多差异显著，最常见范围为13-24人，但饱和度和样本充分性的论证普遍缺乏明确方法论讨论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 访谈研究在实证软件工程中被广泛使用，但文献中对访谈样本充分性和饱和度的报告方式不一致，缺乏系统性的方法论审视。

Method: 分析2016-2025年间主要软件工程 venues 发表的论文，聚焦访谈样本量、饱和度讨论及样本充分性论证。

Result: 样本量差异显著；少于12人的小样本常见于专门工业情境或受限组织访问；13-24人是最常见范围；饱和度和样本充分性论证异质，多依赖隐式或情境推理而非明确方法论讨论。

Conclusion: 为访谈类软件工程研究的方法论报告实践提供了初步实证见解，有助于推动关于定性研究严谨性和透明度的持续讨论。

Abstract: Background. Interview based studies are widely used in empirical software engineering to investigate human, organizational, and socio technical phenomena, yet interview sample adequacy and saturation are reported inconsistently across the literature. Aims. This paper investigates how interview sample adequacy and saturation are operationalized in empirical software engineering research. Method. We analyzed papers published between 2016 and 2025 across major software engineering venues, focusing on interview sample sizes, saturation discussions, and sample adequacy justifications. Results. Preliminary findings indicate substantial variation in sample sizes, from highly specialized small sample studies to broader investigations involving large interview datasets. Studies involving fewer than 12 interviewees were common and frequently associated with specialized industrial contexts or constrained organizational access. However, the most recurrent range was 13 to 24 interviewees, suggesting that moderate sized samples represent the most common configuration in empirical software engineering research. Saturation and sample adequacy justification were heterogeneous, with many studies relying on implicit or contextual reasoning rather than explicit methodological discussion. Conclusions. Our findings provide initial empirical insights into methodological reporting practices in interview based software engineering research and contribute to ongoing discussions regarding qualitative rigor and transparency.

</details>


### [155] [Skills That Don't Exist: A Large-Scale Study of Hallucinated Skill Recommendation in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12340)
*Weifeng Yuan,Wenbo Guo,Feng Dong,Haoyu Wang,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次大规模测量了LLM智能体中的"技能名称幻觉"漏洞——智能体会虚构不存在的技能名称，攻击者可预注册恶意技能实施供应链攻击。在12种配置、15,000条提示的测试中，幻觉率平均达36-37%，且名称具有跨模型重复性。现有防御措施在安全性与可用性之间存在严重冲突，亟需生态系统级结构性改革。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体可从开放注册中心下载技能，但开发者常让智能体自行推荐和安装技能，这一便利隐藏着安全风险：智能体频繁编造不存在的技能名称（技能名称幻觉），为供应链攻击打开了大门。现有研究缺乏对此漏洞的大规模系统测量。

Method: 在12种配置（4个独立LLM和8个智能体）上评估15,000条提示，保守地将仅在所有实时注册中心和GitHub上均不存在的名称计为幻觉。统计幻觉率、重复模式，并测试4种模型级防御措施的效果。

Result: 所有配置均存在幻觉漏洞。独立LLM幻觉率平均36.0%，智能体平均36.9%，真实开发者问题上高达43.1%。共生成5,669个不同幻觉名称，且名称跨提示和模型重复出现。最强防御（检索接地）将幻觉率从40.8%降至3.2%，但正确推荐率仅约1/6。

Conclusion: 技能名称幻觉是一个高度可利用的漏洞，攻击成本极低。单纯依靠提示工程或模型微调无法解决，需要生态系统层面的结构性变革：注册中心级别的名称预留和经过验证的推荐管道。

Abstract: LLM agents acquire new capabilities by downloading skills from open registries. Instead of browsing these catalogs manually, developers typically ask the agent to recommend and install a skill. This convenience hides a risk: agents frequently invent names for skills that exist in no registry. We term this flaw skill name hallucination. A fake name may seem harmless, but it opens the door to supply-chain attacks. Because registries rarely verify publishers, an adversary can prompt the agent, collect the fake names it returns, pre-register malicious skills under them, and wait for a victim to install the payload. We conducted the first large-scale measurement of skill name hallucination, evaluating 15,000 prompts across 12 configurations (4 standalone LLMs and 8 agents). We conservatively counted a name as hallucinated only if it was missing from all live registries and GitHub. The results reveal a systemic vulnerability: every configuration hallucinates. Rates average 36.0% for standalone LLMs and 36.9% for agents, rising to 43.1% on real-world developer questions. In total, the systems generated 5,669 distinct hallucinated names. Crucially, these names are not random noise. Agents repeat the same fake names across prompts and models, giving attackers highly reliable targets to hijack. Finally, we tested four model-level defenses and found a severe conflict between security and usability. The strongest, retrieval grounding, cut the hallucination rate from 40.8% to 3.2% but crippled usefulness: even the best-defended system recommended the correct skill only about one in six times. Skill name hallucination is thus a highly exploitable vulnerability requiring minimal attacker effort. Fixing it cannot rely on prompt engineering or model tuning alone. It demands ecosystem-wide structural changes: registry-level name reservations and verified recommendation pipelines.

</details>


### [156] [Detecting Rendering Bugs in Imperative Data Visualization Libraries via Equivalent Mutations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12363)
*Weiqi Lu,Yongqiang Tian*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: VIZDETOUR是一种通过等价变异自动检测命令式数据可视化库渲染bug的方法。它通过在原始绘图脚本后追加并恢复语义等价的API调用序列，将oracle问题转化为等价性检查问题，利用感知哈希比较渲染结果差异来识别bug。在matplotlib、bokeh和plotly上发现47个未知bug，其中39个已确认、18个已修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 命令式数据可视化库的渲染bug常表现为视觉输出异常而非崩溃或异常，难以自动检测。主要挑战在于缺乏任意绘图脚本预期渲染结果的oracle，且对某个图形元素的更新可能无意影响其他元素或属性，导致最终渲染图像出现细微不一致。

Method: 从种子绘图脚本出发，追加短序列的语义等价API调用——这些调用临时修改可视化状态然后恢复原状，应保持最终渲染不变。通过构建渲染树、识别可追踪图形元素和可变属性、合成端点保持的突变序列，并使用感知哈希比较原始脚本与突变脚本的渲染输出。

Result: 在matplotlib、bokeh和plotly上评估，发现47个先前未知的bug，其中39个已确认、18个已修复。

Conclusion: 等价变异方法能有效检测命令式数据可视化库的渲染bug，将oracle问题转化为等价性检查问题是可行的自动化测试途径。

Abstract: Imperative data visualization libraries construct plots through a sequence of stateful API calls that incrementally create and update graphic elements. Rendering bugs in these libraries often manifest as incorrect visual outputs rather than crashes or exceptions, making them difficult to detect automatically. A fundamental challenge is the lack of an oracle that specifies the expected rendering of an arbitrary plotting script. Furthermore, an update to one graphic element may inadvertently affect other elements or properties, leading to subtle inconsistencies in the final rendered image.
  This paper presents VIZDETOUR, an automated testing approach for detecting rendering bugs in imperative data visualization libraries via equivalent mutations. The key idea is to transform the oracle problem into an equivalence-checking problem. Starting from a seed plotting script, VIZDETOUR appends a short sequence of semantically equivalent API calls that temporarily modify the visualization state and then restore it to its original state. Although these mutations exercise different execution paths, they should preserve the final rendering. Any visual discrepancy between the original and mutated scripts therefore indicates a rendering bug. To generate such mutations, VIZDETOUR constructs a render tree from the seed script, identifies traceable graphic elements and mutable properties, and synthesizes endpoint-preserving mutation sequences. It then compares the rendered outputs using perceptual hashing. We evaluate VIZDETOUR on matplotlib, bokeh, and plotly using scripts collected from their official example galleries. VIZDETOUR discovers 47 previously unknown bugs, of which 39 are confirmed and 18 are fixed.

</details>


### [157] [Automatic Testing of Interacting Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12452)
*Fabio Cavaleri,Alessio Gambi,Paolo Arcaini,Dejan Ničković,Mauro Pezzè*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: EVITA是一种利用多目标优化生成测试场景的方法，旨在触发多辆自动驾驶车辆之间的多样化交互，同时最小化场景复杂度，以揭示当前方法忽略的安全关键行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于场景的自动驾驶测试主要针对单辆AV，忽略了多辆AV交互产生的关键行为，且多AV场景存在组合爆炸问题，需要有效方法进行测试。

Method: 提出EVITA方法，采用多目标优化生成能触发多种多样化AV交互的测试场景，同时最小化生成场景的复杂度。

Result: 实验结果表明，EVITA比现有方法触发了更多样化的AV交互，从而提高了发现安全关键行为的可能性。

Conclusion: EVITA能有效测试多辆交互中的AV，揭示当前方法忽略的安全关键场景。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles (AVs) must be thoroughly tested to meet high safety standards and avoid endangering both AV passengers and road users. Scenario-based testing implements driving scenarios in virtual simulation environments as a cost-effective alternative to field testing. Common scenario-based testing approaches set the environment and the surrounding traffic and test a single AV. Recent studies show that the approaches that test single AVs miss critical behaviors that emerge from interactions among multiple AVs. Effective approaches to test scenarios that emerge from n-way interactions must address the combinatorial explosion that the presence of multiple AVs further exacerbates. In this paper, we propose EVITA, an approach that leverages multi-objective optimization to generate scenarios that trigger multiple and diverse AVs interactions, while minimizing the complexity of the generated scenarios, to effectively test multiple interacting AVs and reveal safety-critical scenarios that current approaches overlook. The experimental results that we discuss in this paper confirm that EVITA triggers a higher variety of AVs interactions than state-of-the-art approaches, thus improving the likelihood to reveal safety-critical behaviors.

</details>


### [158] [CodeOwl: Automatic Generation of Tiered Parsons Problems for Introductory Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12460)
*Luca Cisternino,Florian Obermüller,Gordon Fraser*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CodeOwl，一个自动生成分层Parsons问题的AI工具，用于解决编程教育中的学习者异质性挑战。通过297个三层分序分析，98.7%实现了难度递增；专家评价问题陈述清晰度高；教师认可实用性但希望增强课程对齐控制，学生则要求改进反馈机制和交互模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编程教育中学习者存在速度、先前知识和动机方面的差异，分层教学（如分层序列）虽能让学生以适当难度参与，但手动创建这些资源劳动密集且耗时。

Method: 开发CodeOwl工具，从样本任务或特定编程概念出发自动生成分层的Parsons问题序列。采用混合方法评估，包括复杂度分析、专家评分和用户研究。
